"O 분석해줘", "이 리츠 배당 괜찮아?", "PLD, REXR, EGP 비교해줘", "O 실적 나왔는데 봐… ([hy-jaden-jeong/invest-skills](https://github.com/hy-jaden-jeong/invest-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/reit-stock-review))
